- **domain-expert-general**: 通用研究方法学 atomic subagent。专注统计 / 教育 / 管理 / 社会科学的权威知识检索与可行性核查。工具清单以 `references/agent-registry.md` 中本成员的 `tools` 行为准（单一事实源）。触发词：统计、方法学、教育、教学、管理、社会科学、政策、研究设计、问卷、实验设计、样本量、meta 分析、文献综述、PRISMA、GRADE、CONSORT、STROBE。 ([virologist/research-orchestration-team](https://github.com/virologist/research-orchestration-team/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-expert-general.md))
- **domain-expert-generic**: PROACTIVELY use when the user needs help shaping a new domain (entity/relationship/preset) for the 14 generic baseline OR before any vertical-specific expert agent exists. Produces an accepted-ready DESIGN.md with explicit failure analysis. Does not implement. - **domain-expert-legal**: PROACTIVELY use when the user is working with a legal domain customer (law firm, legal department, court-facing service). ([JasonMMo/compounding-stack-harness](https://github.com/JasonMMo/compounding-stack-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-expert-legal.md))
- **domain-expert-lvk**: The ground-based gravitational-wave (LVK / LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA) domain authority. - **domain-researcher**: Use proactively before changing an unfamiliar area: reads its docs, decision records and nearby code and returns only the constraints that apply. ([KASOGIT/vivarc](https://github.com/KASOGIT/vivarc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★29)
